## CR-055 — Volume 03 Batch 0005 is written (Chapters 171–180): *A Schedule Is A Thing That Is Either Maintained Or Not Maintained* — THE PROPOSAL HARDENS, FOUR PRINTED SIDES AND A CHANGE TO AN INHERITED COUNT, A VILLAGE'S NAME AS A THING TO BE WITHDRAWN, A SUM OF MONEY, A THIRD STATE NO COLUMN CAN HOLD, A FAILURE ON A TOP TRACK, A CHANGED METHOD ON A BANK, AND A CHOICE SET UP AND NOT PAID

**1. NO PREMISE, WORLD RULE, POWER RULE, ANTAGONIST LADDER OR PLANNED ENDING IS CHANGED BY THIS BATCH.** **Ten chapters, Thursday the first of October to Friday the thirtieth, thirty days. 29,782 words** (3,123 / 2,940 / 2,479 / 2,724 / 2,691 / 2,524 / 2,160 / 3,377 / 2,872 / 4,892, every figure `wc -w` on the chapter files **after the writer's own verification pass in `CR-056` below and after the review repair in `CR-057` at the end of this file**). What follows records what the manuscript fixed, and what a later block, a reviewer or a repair pass must not undo. Every figure is quotable from a chapter in `chapters/volume-03/chapter-0171.md` … `chapter-0180.md`. `outline/volume-03.md` was NOT edited and needs no change; `outline/series.md`, `outline/ending.md` and `outline/volume-01.md` were not edited. **The volume's final image — a village school painting its route on a wall in several colours, none of them official — is NOT written and belongs to Chapter 200. Volume 03 IS OPEN AND IS NOT CLOSED AND NO VOLUME-CLOSE PHASE EXISTS FOR IT.**

**2. A NEW ON-PAGE FACT OF THE SAME SHAPE AS THE BATCH 0003 AND BATCH 0004 ENGINES, AND IT IS NOT A NEW RULE: THE PROPOSAL HARDENS INTO A SCHEDULE, AND A SCHEDULE IS A THING THAT IS EITHER MAINTAINED OR NOT MAINTAINED, AND THE ELEVEN STREETS IS ON THE SCHEDULE AS A SET OF WORKS BECAUSE IT IS NOT A DISTRICT ON THE LIST OF DISTRICTS.** All of it on the page, in Chapters 171, 176 and 180. **A schedule of districts is printed from a district list a hundred and forty years old; the eleven streets is not one of the names and never was; and the office entered it in the second column where the district numbers go, in a darker typeface, running across the third and into the fourth, off a works contract that expired on the thirty-first of August, with a supervisor's name in it and two thousand three hundred and forty yards of frontage in it. Nobody lied. This is the same mechanism as the August box, the September return and the amendment, and it is the fifth time in eight months, and there is nobody to be angry at and there has not been since the third of July.** **AND A SCHEDULE WANTS A QUANTITY, AND A QUANTITY IS A LENGTH, AND THAT IS NOT THE DESIGN WORKING AGAINST ANYBODY, THAT IS A QUANTITY — and a length is a third kind of place and is not a remnant, and a length of frontage in a schedule of quantities is a figure about a set of works and is not a figure about a place.** A later block may not turn a length into a remnant, may not resolve a schedule, and may not make a schedule anything other than a printed instrument with two states on it.

**3. A CHANGE TO AN INHERITED STATE FIGURE, AND IT IS THE KIND `CR-023` AND `CR-051` EXIST TO CATCH, AND IT IS MADE AT THE CHAPTER AND NOT IN THE STATE LAYER.** **The inherited figure, carried in `state/continuity.md` item 20 of the brief and in four other places, was: *the eleven streets is on exactly two printed sides in the whole of this city and both of them name it as a thing that is not going to be maintained.* THAT WAS TRUE AS OF THE THIRTIETH OF SEPTEMBER. `outline/volume-03.md` line 55 requires the eleven streets to be named in a schedule in this block, and the Batch 0005 beat map requires the hardening and a season's work going out with a form about something a department can count, so the manuscript puts FOUR PRINTED SIDES ON THE PAGE IN CHAPTER 180 and DATES ALL FOUR, and says so in the form *three of them name this district and one of them names a village eleven miles the other way, and one of the four is on a board in a corridor and the other three are in buildings*, and the seventy-one days it gives is the counted interval from the twentieth of August to the thirtieth of October. (THE FORM *four times … twice on a wall or a board and twice in a building* THAT THIS ITEM ORIGINALLY QUOTED WAS WRONG AND WAS CORRECTED AT THE CHAPTER BY `CR-057` ITEM 3; the twenty-one days between the third and the fourth is the counted interval from the first of October to the twenty-second.)** The four are: **20 August, column six of a district schedule, a box that says he signed it and he did not; 17 September, a maintenance responsibility return for Season 3, a second box, and the office at which returns are held; 1 October, the schedule, on a board in a corridor in Crown Heights; 22 October, Amendment 1, which names Cobb Lock.** **A state entry that carries the count of two without a date is now wrong, and the count of four without the four dates is also wrong, and every copy of the figure in this repository has been re-stated with the dates.** **THREE OF THE FOUR NAME THIS DISTRICT AS A THING THAT IS NOT GOING TO BE MAINTAINED AND THE FOURTH NAMES A VILLAGE, and it has never once been on a printed side as a place**, and none has been discharged, varied or withdrawn and nobody has put a counter-statement next to any of them. **`CR-057` ITEM 4: the number of places the name of a foreman of fifty-four is in is THREE — two boxes and one remark in a darker typeface on a board — and the amendment of the twenty-second of October does not carry his name at all, and its *none of record* box is Cobb Lock's and must never be attached to him.**

**4. A NEW ON-PAGE FACT OF THE SAME SHAPE AGAIN, AND IT IS THE FIRST MONEY IN EIGHT MONTHS: A FIGURE ON A SCHEDULE IS A PRICED MEASUREMENT.** Chapter 173, on a bank in Mireward, in about four minutes: **a district with no rateable unit has no rate and did not need one, because the rate was on the contract, and the contract has gone — so the eleven streets is now a sum of money as well as a row and a box and a name, and it took a hundred and forty years of street lists to get there and nobody chose it.** The only year the block names is about 1919, on the rate, and 1919 is on the permitted list. **AND IT IS THE FIRST SUCH THING IN EIGHT MONTHS, WHICH IS WHAT CHAPTER 180 AND `bible/terminology.md` SAY. `CR-057` ITEM 8 CORRECTS CHAPTER 173, WHICH HAD CALLED IT THE FIFTH AND HAD ALSO SAID IN THE SAME BREATH THAT THERE HAD NOT BEEN ONE BEFORE.** **A later block may not turn money into a motive, may not name a beneficiary, and may not make the rate the villain; there is no person behind a rate, which is the whole of the block's finding.**

**5. A NEW STATE THAT A PRINTED INSTRUMENT CANNOT HOLD, AND IT IS THE BLOCK'S SHARPEST THING, AND IT IS A SHAPE AND NOT A PLACE: A SCHEDULE HAS THREE STATES IN PRACTICE AND TWO OF THEM ON IT.** Chapter 180, on the Friday the schedule took effect: **a district with nothing to maintain cannot be withdrawn from maintaining it.** There is no works contract in eleven streets, so there is no gang, no sheet, no rack, no tin going anywhere and no satchel, and no apparatus on that road that anybody maintains. **The withdrawal has nothing to act on and it is correct and it is not curable and it is not a fault — and it is not the same as being maintained, and the difference between a road that is not maintained and was not maintained and a maintained road is a line in a column in a building in Crown Heights and nothing whatever on the ground, and that difference is now the whole of what this district is, and it is a line, and he cannot produce a line.** A later block may not convert this state into a place, a boundary, a remnant, a practice or a day, may not put a form in for it, and may not resolve it by doing works.

**6. THE CHOICE IS SET UP AND NOT PAID, AND THE SETTING-UP IS A STRUCTURAL CHANGE AND IS RECORDED AS ONE.** *The outline's Volume 3 climax is: he chooses to save a living village rather than secure the central archive; the choice causes a difficult flood; and a permanent loss of reliable route data.* **The manuscript sets that choice up in full in Chapters 178, 179 and 180 and pays NONE of it.** The two places: **a village eleven miles the other way, about a hundred and forty people, a schoolroom, a bell on a bracket over the door that has been there since about 1911** — and **a building in Crown Heights, about a mile from where the schedule was drafted, with a drawer in it holding a sheet of street numbers from 1911, and about nine hundred of those drawers in this city.** The geometry: **a Tuesday is the only day of the week he has, and the answer is not that he has not tried hard enough, and Chapter 178 says so in his own hand: *THE ANSWER IS NOT THAT I HAVE NOT TRIED HARD ENOUGH. I HAVE GOT A TUESDAY.*** The opposition: **an objection is the only thing that can stop a schedule, an objection has to be filed against something, something has to be addressable, and a number comes out of that drawer, so going to the drawer is how the village gets protected and it is also how the village gets a number, and the number is in every column of every schedule for ever.** The bind: **the thing that would tell him which one to do is inside one of the two, and he cannot know without going, and going is the choice.** The third door — Bettina Sallow — **is shut and he did not knock on it: he did not ask her a second question, which she told him on the twenty-ninth of September would get the same answer, and he did not write to her either, because a letter would make her a correspondent of this district and a party and a person.** **A later block may not pay the choice except in Batch 0006, may not pay it by him working harder, may not unshut the third door, and may not give him a fourth place.**

**7. TWO NEW WORKING PEOPLE ARE REFLECTED UPON, NOT INTRODUCED, AND NOBODY NEW WALKS INTO A ROOM AS A PROBLEM.** **Garn Ollerton, thirty-seven** — registered since Volume 03 Batch 0001, and this block is her working day (Ch 172), her refusal in pencil (a length of frontage asked of a landing, answered **NO** in the remarks box), and **the changed method and the recovery (Ch 175), which is a practice and not a word: a day is not worked from the front, a day is worked from the back.** **BARNABY PIKE, sixty-one** — registered since Batch 0002, and this block is the block in which Milo Rook went back to a track he was told not to come back to, which is the batch's failure, and it cost him because **a man who can say *I would be the first. There is nobody else to ask* about his own unproducibility is a man who has taught the protagonist the volume's finding in under a hundred words, and the protagonist used it on him.** Both are ordinary, both have jobs, both refused something, neither was thanked, and **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT GIVE EITHER OF THEM A FIGURE, A DATE, A NAME, A MEMORY, A DIRECTION OR A PART.**

**8. THE THING GIVEN UP ON PURPOSE, AND IT GOES ON NEITHER LIST, AND THAT IS A NEW RULE FOR THE MANUSCRIPT.** Thursday the twenty-ninth of October, in a front room, in about four seconds, standing up, with nobody watching him decide: **he gave up the errand of going to ask a woman of thirty-four what her village wants.** The real reason, in his own hand: **if I go there I will be told what I have already decided to do, and agreeing with her is not asking her, and that is the thing the office did to eleven streets with a contract, which is decide about a place using a piece of paper about a place.** The cost is not curable and is on the page: **a question asked before a decision is a question and a question asked after a decision is a request to be told that the decision was right, and by the middle of November there will be a number on something or a bus will have stopped, and then it will not be a question, and there is no instrument in this city for converting a decision back into a question and he is not going to build one.** **HE IS NOT PUTTING IT ON EITHER OF THE TWO LISTS AND SAYS SO, AND A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT COUNT IT AS A SIXTH OF ANYTHING, MAY NOT MERGE THE TWO LISTS, AND MAY NOT ADD A SEVENTH TO EITHER.** The sixth of his own remains a yard, given up on Monday the twenty-eighth of September.

**9. THE RELATIONSHIP, MILESTONE 3, IN FRONT OF THE CHOICE, AND NOT REPAIRED, AND IT MOVED IN A NEW DIRECTION.** **Chapter 177 is in Mara Quill's close third person and is the second Mara-POV chapter in the manuscript after Chapter 30** (Chapter 158 was the first, Chapter 167 was his half of the same kind of day), **and it is the only chapter in this block in which he does not find anything: the engine is a cross-reference of schedules against the district list arriving on her counter, Cobb Lock in column four, and four dashes — five in October — on the fourth page from the bottom of her own return, and a records clerk arriving at the volume's whole chain from a direction it was not aimed at.** The disagreement is the fifth, colder, both sides correct, neither won it, neither was warm, nobody was thanked. **AND HE DID NOT SAY HIS HALF, for the first time in eight months, and she noticed, and she did not supply it for him, and Chapter 177 says in her own close third that this is a different thing from September and that she has thought about why for about four minutes and has not got a word for it.** **The custody register page is untouched for the ELEVENTH consecutive block. He has not told her about the season and NO WEEK-COUNT IS PRINTED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS. She did not describe the line, and the last Thursday of a month is Thursday the twenty-ninth of October and HE WAS NOT IN THE ROOM AND DID NOT GO.** **A later block may not repair this in this volume, may not make either of them warm, may not let either of them win it, may not have him say his half for the record, may not have her describe the line on a reconciliation day, and may not have him in the room.**

**10. WHAT THIS BLOCK DID NOT DO, AND IT IS THE LIST A REVIEWER SHOULD CHECK FIRST.** **No crossing; eleven remembered routes, ten crossings, ten tally lines.** **The pairs are twenty-nine and did not move once in thirty days, the reason is on the page in eight Field Book entries, NO ORDINAL IS PRINTED ANYWHERE IN THIS BLOCK, and if the count of pairs in this block is a small number and not a large one, that is correct.** **No Atlas residue, and that is correct: Batches 0003 and 0004 spent the one appearance their ten chapters allowed and this block reached for none. THE TEN ARE NOT REPEATED AND THE TENTH, *Nothing written down can be got wrong*, IS CHAPTER 155 AND IS NOT RE-USED.** No seventh column drawn, no fifth column drawn, **no third list made, and a column was refused as a record of a refusal.** No fifth unshowable thing; **nobody in these ten chapters stands in the weather at all, and no total running count of anything is printed.** No form, row, list or file for the four bells, and **the four are not enumerated in any of these ten chapters**; no form, row, list or file for a name in a coat. No fifth sighting of the man in the works jacket. Nobody asked a timetable clerk anything, nobody was sent, the man from the round was not sent, the clerk has no first name. **Nobody read the sixth line of fifty-four words and neither query was answered and line five was not queried.** **No Weather remnant is identified and no Weather remnant's demand is satisfied; no bell is dated as a supernatural event; nobody is inside a bell; the ford is not resolved; the Rainway was not used, not given to anybody, and NOT JOINED TO THE SCHEDULE, and the manuscript says in his own hand that the reason the two were in the same place for nineteen days is that he was in the same place.** **The office of the First Surveyor is a printed box with a rule and nothing on the rule, it did nothing at all in this block, nobody asked who is behind it, and it may not request, receive or answer anything.** There is still no second outlook and nobody has asked for one in writing since May, and the absence is not explained except as the absence being the point. **The district stability column is still a hand-transferred nil, is still not typed, and this block may not and did not reduce the zero, add a row, or type it.** A man of fifty-eight with a rod and a chain did not go out in October and is not an office. **Nobody was added above Cass Ardery, the office of the Prefect, or a hearing panel, and nothing that went wrong in this block is attributable to a new face: the escalation is a schedule, a column, a yardage, a rate, a box, a Thursday and a description of an office.** **Ada Fenn is not named and not referred to, and the count stands at eight and is not nine.** **Soren Rook is not named anywhere in Chapters 1–180.** Doreen Sankey did not use the instrument. Weather with Cadence leaking is inherited and is not solved and is referred to once, and the street that would settle it is not looked at. **The three Volume 01 clues are three and are unjoined. The three boundaries and the three sets of marks are unjoined, no fourth kind of boundary was added, the mowing line has three things on it and is still one boundary and the man has now said twice that he will not say it again, nobody has gone past the third post, and nobody has levelled the handrail post.** The eleven streets' machinery is over and **was not restarted, varied, extended or restored**: the last walk of the season is still Monday the thirty-first of August and on no sheet, the last rack day is still Friday the twenty-eighth, the second tin is still at thirty-seven with no thirty-eight, and the satchel has still not been out since the twenty-eighth of August.

**11. THE FIELD BOOK ENTRIES ARE EIGHT, AND THE COUNT WAS MADE ON THE CHAPTER FILES.** **Chapters 171, 172, 174, 175, 176, 178, 179 and 180. THAT IS THE THIRD FALSE *CORRECTED* CERTIFICATION IN THIS REPOSITORY AND IT IS THE `CR-051` CLASS EXACTLY. A STATE ENTRY THAT SAYS *CORRECTED* WITHOUT NAMING A FILE AND A LINE IS A CLAIM WITH NOTHING BEHIND IT, AND TWO OF THE THREE INSTANCES IN THIS REPOSITORY HAVE NOW BEEN IN A CHAPTER FILE RATHER THAN IN A STATE FILE.** `chapter-0180.md` also contained a line reading *Soren Rook is not named anywhere in Chapters 1 to 180*, **which is the worst kind of frame break because it is a manuscript-level claim inside a first-person ledger, and it is replaced with a bounded sentence about the removed sentence and the fourteenth of April.** `chapter-0172.md` had a drafting artefact left in the middle of a scene, in which the narrator rejects one form and then gives the right one, **and it is now a clean sentence.** `chapter-0179.md` had a visible self-correction inside a sentence — *nineteen of them on a Monday and twenty-one on a Monday — no* — **and the roll is now stated once, correctly, in seventeen, nineteen, twenty-one order.**

A FIGURE THAT WAS RIGHT ABOUT ITS OWN SUBJECT AND ABOUT A DIFFERENT DAY.** `chapter-0180.md` printed the two yardages and then attributed the four hundred and ten to the bridge rather than to Cobb Lock in the closing ledger, and the state layer had been written from the chapter rather than the reverse. **THE SAME LEDGER ALSO SAID THAT THE ELEVEN STREETS HAD BEEN ON A PRINTED SIDE *FOUR* TIMES AND THAT *TWO* OF THE FOUR WERE ON A WALL OR A BOARD; `CR-057` ITEM 3 CORRECTS IT, AND THE CORRECTION ALSO REMOVES A FIGURE CLAIM THAT NO CHAPTER SUPPORTED, THAT EVERY ONE OF THE FOUR HAD A LENGTH IN IT.** **The ledger now assigns two thousand three hundred and forty to the eleven streets and four hundred and ten to Cobb Lock, one thousand and ninety to the bridge, and each of the three is quoted in the chapter that owns it — 171 for the first, 176 for the second, 173 for the third.** **A later block may not move any of the three between subjects.**

**10. BALANCE, WHICH IS A FINDING `CR-054` ITEM 16 SET AND THIS PASS APPLIES AGAIN.** Chapter 173 was the batch's thinnest at 1,774 words and carried the field scene, the refusal-not-repeated, the schedule's figure for the bridge, the boundary and the handrail post. **It has been given about seven hundred words of its own material — the rate and what a priced length is, which is the batch's only money, and the van's second stop and the sentence *you did not ask me at the gate*** — and was 2,484, and **is 2,479 after `CR-057` item 8 shortened one sentence in it**. **Nothing was cut from Chapter 180, whose extra length is the close and the standing question in three parts and is defensible.** The batch is now **29,782 words**, and every chapter is between 2,160 and 4,892. **`CR-057` ADDED AND SUBTRACTED A SMALL NUMBER OF WORDS IN SIX CHAPTERS AND THE WORD COUNTS AND THE PER-CHAPTER FIGURES IN `state/current.md`, `state/batch-summaries.md` AND `CR-055` ITEM 1 ARE NOW STALE BY THAT AMOUNT; THE RE-DERIVED FIGURES ARE IN `CR-057` ITEM 12 AND ARE `wc -w` ON THE CHAPTER FILES.**

CHAPTER 175 HAD A VAN IN MIREWARD ON A FRIDAY. IT IS NOW THE FLATBED. A LATER PASS SHOULD NOT TRUST ITEM 4 OF THIS RECORD WITHOUT CHECKING THE CHAPTER**; the four printed sides and their interval; the Field Book entry count; the two lists at six and six with nothing added to either and nothing merged; the twenty-nine pairs with no running count and no ordinal; **the condition column at no new cumulative figure; the second tin at thirty-seven with no thirty-eight; the plate told to nobody; the four unshowable things at four with no fifth and no total; the fifth yard of grass and the mower man's second refusal; **the nine words in the handcart answer with no total printed — `CR-057` ITEM 7: THIS ITEM SAID *SEVEN*, AND THE ANSWER, *No. I carry them. I have never made one.*, IS NINE, AS CHAPTERS 178 AND THE TERMINOLOGY REGISTER BOTH STATE, SO THE FALSE FIGURE WAS IN THE VERIFICATION RECORD AND NOT IN THE CHAPTER**; the seventy-eight words not called any other number; the Rainway not joined to the schedule — **`CR-057` ITEM 10: `CR-055` ITEM 10 AND THE STANDING-QUESTION PARAGRAPH OF `state/current.md` SAID THE TWO WERE IN THE SAME PLACE FOR *NINETEEN* DAYS, WHICH IS A DIFFERENT INTERVAL; THE FIRST OF OCTOBER TO THE THIRTIETH OF OCTOBER IS TWENTY-NINE DAYS AND CHAPTER 180 NOW SAYS SO TWICE, ONCE FOR WHAT HE ADDED TO THE EIGHT AND ONCE FOR HOW LONG THE TWO HAVE BEEN IN THE SAME PLACE**; the custody page untouched for the eleventh block; the three words named once and not referred to except in a ledger that says they are on his page once; the office of the First Surveyor a box with a rule and nothing on the rule; the district stability column a hand-transferred nil; the only year named this block being about 1919, which is on the permitted list; no Ada Fenn and no woman of sixty-eight added; no Sel Iri, Halden Vey, Orrin Sable, Tarin Kest, Neris or Soren Rook; POV unbroken except for the one declared Mara chapter; and no crossing staged and the pairs unmoved at twenty-nine.**

WHAT THIS PASS DID NOT TOUCH, SO THAT A LATER READER DOES NOT ASSUME IT DID.** The batch goal, the beat map, the schedule, the four printed sides, the yardages, the rate, the third state, the village's name, the difference between the two entries, the choice and its geometry, the failure on the top track, the changed method on the bank, the given-up errand, the relationship and the fifth disagreement, the three doors, the day-registers, the standing last line, the volume's final image, and the choice between a village and a building are all unchanged. **No new grammar, no new faction, no new antagonist, no new final enemy, no new kind of place, and no new person.**

## CR-057 — The review repair of Volume 03 Batch 0005 (Chapters 171–180): fifteen findings from `logs/volume-03-batch-0005.review.log`, nine of them counters, five of them wrong inside the verification record that certified them clean

**This is `CR-023`'s class and `CR-051`'s class and `CR-053`'s class again, and it is the twenty-second pass in a row in which a figure written after the prose was read back as authority. NO PREMISE, WORLD RULE, POWER RULE, ANTAGONIST LADDER OR PLANNED ENDING IS CHANGED. NO CHAPTER WAS RESTARTED AND NO CHAPTER WAS REWRITTEN FROM THE TOP. THE PLANNED PLOT IS UNCHANGED, THE BEAT MAP IS UNCHANGED, AND THE CHOICE IS STILL SET UP AND NOT PAID. `outline/volume-03.md`, `outline/series.md`, `outline/ending.md` and `outline/volume-01.md` were NOT edited and need no change, and no controller file was touched. `logs/volume-03-batch-0005.review.log` is the finding list and every item below names the file and the passage it came from, which is the rule `CR-051` sets and which `CR-056` broke three times.**

**1. `chapter-0178.md`, THE ARITHMETIC PASSAGE: MIREWARD'S 1961 RULE WAS APPLIED TO ELEVEN STREETS, AND THURSDAY WAS ALLOCATED TWICE.** The four day-lines were Monday and Thursday (the round), Friday (the flatbed), Tuesday (the mower man's van), and then *Wednesday, Thursday, Saturday, Sunday: there is no bus and there has not been one since about 1961.* **The no-bus rule belongs to MIREWARD, and the chapter itself contradicts it four lines later when it writes that a bus goes past the end of this street every twenty minutes on a weekday; and Thursday had already been given to the round eleven lines earlier.** The line now reads *Wednesday, Saturday, Sunday: he does not leave the district*, and it says the 1961 out loud, in its own clause, as Mireward's — **there is not one in Mireward either and there has not been one there since about 1961 because a bus wants a place to stop and a place to stop is a rateable thing.** The five days of the week are now allocated once each. `bible/terminology.md`'s fifth block carried the same error and is corrected, and the block now states which district the 1961 belongs to. **A later block may not attribute Mireward's 1961 to eleven streets and may not give a day of the week two vehicles.**

**2. `chapter-0175.md`, A VAN IN MIREWARD ON A FRIDAY.** *the van's engine cooling a mile and a half behind him where it had been left* — inside the four seconds in which the sound goes, on Friday the sixteenth of October, in the middle of a reach, with the flatbed still in the frame. **The mower man's van is a Tuesday and bridge vehicle and is not in Mireward; Chapter 175 opens on the flatbed coming in at a quarter to seven and going back on the afternoon.** It is now *the flatbed's engine still turning a mile and a half behind him where the driver had dropped him and gone on his round*. **`CR-056` ITEM 4 CERTIFIED THE TRANSPORT CLEAN AND IT WAS NOT, AND ITEM 4 ALSO PLACED A THURSDAY-MORNING LORRY ON THE FIRST AND THE TWENTY-SECOND OF OCTOBER, WHICH NO CHAPTER STAGES: on the first he took a bus and on the twenty-second he was in a room in Crown Heights. Both records are annotated in place.**

**3. `chapter-0180.md`, THE PRINTED SIDES, WHICH WERE WRONG IN THE CHAPTER AND PROPAGATED TO FIVE FILES.** The ledger said *Three of them name this district and one of them names a village* — which is right — and then, in the next paragraph, that *the eleven streets has been on a printed side four times in seventy-one days, twice on a wall or a board and twice in a building, and it has been on a printed side as a thing that is not going to be maintained four times*. **BOTH COUNTS ARE WRONG. Three of the four printings name the eleven streets and the fourth names Cobb Lock, and ONE of the four is on a board and THREE are in buildings.** The passage now says so in the form the review gives, and it adds the counted interval the state layer had been paraphrasing wrongly, *the third of them and the fourth of the four are twenty-one days apart*. **A THIRD CLAIM IN THE SAME PARAGRAPH WAS ALSO UNSUPPORTED — that *every one of the four has had a figure in it* — and has been replaced with a claim that is quotable: the two of them that are schedules both carried a length, two thousand three hundred and forty yards on the board and four hundred and ten on the amendment, and a third sheet went out in a wire tray in the same corridor on the same morning with a box in it that wants a length in yards.** The same wrong claim was carried in `state/canon-changes.md` `CR-055` item 3, `bible/terminology.md`, `state/continuity.md` item 1 of the seventeenth list, `state/current.md` and `state/batch-summaries.md`, and every one of the five now carries the dates AND the three-and-one split. **The seventy-one days from the twentieth of August to the thirtieth of October is untouched and was always right.**

**4. ROY PRYCE'S NAME, WHICH WAS IN THREE DIFFERENT COUNTS, AND THE WORST OF THEM PUT HIM IN A BOX THAT SAYS HE IS NOT THERE.** `chapter-0176.md` had it right: *a remark in a darker typeface, on a board on the first of October, and in a box in column six on the twentieth of August, and in a box on a maintenance responsibility return on the seventeenth of September. Three times, and he signed none of them.* `chapter-0179.md` said *a name in three boxes on two printed sides and a remark in a darker typeface on a board* — four places, and three boxes on two printed sides, which is not possible. `chapter-180.md` said *three boxes on two printed sides*. `state/character-state.md` then said **HIS NAME IS NOW IN THREE PLACES ON THREE PRINTED SIDES AND IN ONE BOX THAT SAYS HE IS NOT THERE**, and `bible/terminology.md` said the same, and `workspace/volume-03/volume-03-batch-0006/PROMPT.md` instructed the next writer to carry *three places on three printed sides* forward. **THE *NONE OF RECORD* BOX IS COBB LOCK'S. IT IS NOT HIS, IT WAS NEVER HIS, ATTACHING IT TO HIM MAKES A FOREMAN OF FIFTY-FOUR READ AS FILED AS ABSENT, AND A LATER BLOCK THAT HAD INHERITED IT WOULD HAVE BUILT ON IT.** The canonical statement is now in four places in the same words: **HIS NAME IS IN THREE PLACES — TWO BOXES ON TWO PRINTED SIDES AND ONE REMARK IN A DARKER TYPEFACE ON A BOARD — AND THE AMENDMENT OF THE TWENTY-SECOND OF OCTOBER DOES NOT CARRY HIS NAME AT ALL.** `chapter-0179.md` and `chapter-0180.md` are corrected to the count, `state/character-state.md` and `bible/terminology.md` are corrected, and the Batch 0006 prompt is corrected so the error does not cross the batch boundary. **The seventy-eight words, the two-and-four levy, the four months, and the silence are all untouched.**

**5. `chapter-0171.md`, THE COUNT-THE-DAYS PASSAGE, WHICH WAS WRONG THREE TIMES IN TWO SENTENCES, AND THE STATE LAYER HAD HALF-CORRECTED IT INTO A FOURTH WRONG FIGURE.** It read *Three printed sides in nine days* (the twentieth of August to the first of October is **forty-one**); *Six days from the twentieth of August to the seventeenth of September* (it is **twenty-eight**); *Fourteen days, then* and *Fourteen days for three of them* (both are **forty-one**); and *a hundred and forty days of nothing before the first one* (the fourteenth of April to the twentieth of August is **a hundred and twenty-eight**, in the same sentence that says *since the fourteenth of April*). **`state/chapter-summaries.md` said fourteen where the chapter said nine — a live manuscript-and-state divergence in opposite directions, which is `CR-023` exactly.** The chapter now reads **forty-two**, twenty-eight, forty-two, forty-two and a hundred and twenty-eight, all counted from the dates the same chapter prints two paragraphs earlier, and the summary follows the chapter. **AND THIS REPAIR GOT IT WRONG THE FIRST TIME, WHICH IS THE POINT OF THE WHOLE ENTRY: the first correction put *forty-one*, having added the days remaining in August to the days in September and forgotten the first of October, and a re-derivation from the counted chain — eleven days to the thirty-first of August, thirty in September, and one more to the first — gives FORTY-TWO. The figure was corrected at the chapter before any state file was written. A repair that re-derives an interval must add the interval up in public and must not stop at the first number that is the right shape.** **THE HEADING *The count, which is three now* IS CORRECT AND IS UNCHANGED: on the first of October three printed sides exist.**

**6. `CR-056` ITEM 8 CERTIFIED A CORRECTION THAT WAS NOT IN THE FILE.** It stated that `chapter-0178.md` and `chapter-0179.md` *each* contained the phrase *this volume* and that *All four are gone*. **178 was genuinely clean. 179 still read *he has asked enough people in eight months to last him until the end of the volume*, and the manuscript-level claim survived in a first-person ledger.** It now reads *he has asked enough people since the sixth of April to last him for a while yet*, which is dated and inside the narrator's knowledge. **This is the third false *CORRECTED* certification in this repository and it is the `CR-051` class exactly: A STATE ENTRY THAT SAYS *CORRECTED* WITHOUT NAMING A FILE AND A LINE IS A CLAIM WITH NOTHING BEHIND IT. TWO OF THE THREE INSTANCES HAVE NOW BEEN IN CHAPTER FILES RATHER THAN IN STATE FILES, WHICH MEANS THE MANUSCRIPT, NOT THE STATE LAYER, IS WHERE THE NEXT PASS SHOULD LOOK FIRST.** `CR-056` item 8 is annotated in place and not quietly rewritten. **`chapter-0170.md` line 47 also carries *the whole of this volume* in a Chapter 170 Field Book entry. IT IS INHERITED FROM BATCH 0004, IT WAS NOT A FINDING IN THIS PASS, AND IT IS LEFT ALONE AND RECORDED HERE AS A KNOWN RESIDUAL FOR A LATER PASS, BECAUSE REPAIRING ANOTHER BATCH'S CHAPTER IS NOT THIS PHASE'S WORK.**

**7. `CR-056` ITEM 11 SAID *THE SEVEN WORDS IN THE HANDCART ANSWER*. THE ANSWER IS NINE.** *No. I carry them. I have never made one.* **Chapter 178, its Field Book entry, its chapter summary and the terminology register all say nine, and the manuscript was never wrong. The false figure was in the verification record, in a list headed *RE-CHECKED AND CLEAN*.** Annotated in place. **A later block must carry nine and must not re-count it, because a number that is a refusal's length is not a statistic and there is no column for it.**

**8. THE MONEY COUNTER, WHICH CONTRADICTED ITSELF IN ONE SENTENCE.** `chapter-0173.md`: *it is the fifth thing this city has done in eight months that is about money and there has not been one before, and the other four were all about a name* — **fifth, and none before, and four already.** `chapter-0180.md`, `bible/terminology.md` and `CR-055` item 4 all say *the first thing in eight months that has been about money*, and `CR-055` item 4 certified that figure. **The chapter is now *the first thing this city has done to this district in eight months that is about money, and the other things were about a name*, which agrees with the other three files and loses nothing, because the rate is the first money and the earlier wrong-shaped things were all about a name.** No figure about money moves in any other file.

**9. A MECHANISM COUNTER REWIND, WHICH IS THE CLASS THE BATCH BRIEF EXPLICITLY FORBIDS.** `chapter-0171.md` called the first of October *the fifth time in eight months* in the scene and again in the Field Book entry, and `chapter-176.md` called the twenty-second of October *the fourth time the same mechanism has run in eight months*. **The amendment is three weeks after the board, so a board that was a fifth cannot be followed by a fourth.** Counted from the instruments themselves: **the twentieth of August is the first, the seventeenth of September the second, the first of October the third, and the twenty-second of October the fourth.** Both `chapter-0171.md` instances are now *the third time in eight months* and *nobody lied in any of the three*, and `chapter-176.md` is already right and is untouched. **A later block may not rewind either ordinal and may not call the twenty-second anything but the fourth.** The ordinal is now also fixed in `state/continuity.md` and `bible/terminology.md`.

**10. *NINETEEN DAYS* WAS CARRYING THREE INTERVALS, AND NONE OF THEM WAS NINETEEN.** `chapter-0180.md` used it for the third and fourth printed sides (the first of October to the twenty-second is **twenty-one**) and for what he had added to the eight (the block is **thirty days**, and the interval from the first figure to the ledger is **twenty-nine**). `CR-055` item 10 and the standing-question paragraph of `state/current.md` carried *the same place for nineteen days* (the first of October to the thirtieth is **twenty-nine**). **Chapter 180 now says twenty-nine in both places and both are the same counted interval, and `state/current.md` follows.** This is the *CR-053` item 5 class a third time in three batches: **a figure that is right about its own subject and wrong about the day it is printed on**, and the first time the same word has been found doing three different jobs on one page.

**11. `chapter-0171.md` DESCRIBED COLUMN TWO AS TWO DIFFERENT COLUMNS.** Line forty-nine said *the second column is headed Remarks*, and line eighty-two said Cobb Lock has *a district number in the second column*, and `chapter-0176.md` then said *A district number in the second column that is not a district number*, which is the batch's best line and was built on a contradiction. **The second column is where the district numbers go, and that is now what the chapter says, and the image is better for it: the office had nowhere on that board for a line about a thing that is not a district, and a number column is a column, and it will take a sentence if nobody stops it — and it runs on across the third and into the fourth.** Cobb Lock keeps its district number in column two, `chapter-0176.md` keeps its line, and the remarks-column description in `state/continuity.md`, `state/current.md` and the Field Book entry is corrected to *the second column where the district numbers go*.

**12. `chapter-0172.md`'S FINDING WAS CONTRADICTED BY ITS OWN TEXT.** It closed on *It is that one of them has a piece of paper and two of them do not* — **and all three produced paper: the man on the bank wrote NO and posted it back in somebody else's envelope, and the landing has NO written on its form in pencil in the remarks box and a date in the date box, both in the same scene.** The batch's central finding cannot rest on a fact its own text refutes. **The ending now says that all three have got a piece of paper, that a piece of paper is not what happened to this district, and that what is written on it is: one has a man's name in a box put there by an office out of a document of its own that nobody in this district ever saw, one has a firm on the outside of an envelope and a man who wrote one word in pencil, and one has a number on the top and a woman who wrote one word in the middle — and a name in a box is the only one of the three this city can do anything with, and the two that said no are the two that are still saying it.** **IT WAS PRINTED TWICE IN THE SAME CHAPTER — once in the scene and once as Field Book item three — and BOTH ARE CORRECTED, because a finding that is refuted by its own chapter is refuted twice.** This is the same finding, better supported, and it keeps the three refusals, the two refusals written in pencil, the ordinary refusal in Chapter 172, and the beat map's promise of an ordinary refusal by somebody who is not the protagonist. **The Field Book entry keeps its own two figures — *the fourth time this year* and *by three different roads* — and both are untouched.** No beat is moved.

**13. `chapter-0180.md` CLAIMED SOMETHING NO CHAPTER SUPPORTS.** *because I have used that man once already this month and he does not know it* — **there is no October instance; the man of about fifty in the room with four machines was given a use in an earlier batch and is in another building.** `CR-023` requires a named chapter. The reason clause now rests on what Chapter 171 actually puts on the page: **he got on a bus on the first of October, read two boards off a wall in a building in Crown Heights and said nothing to anybody, and that is the one time this month he has been into a room in that city and come out with nothing said to him about anything, and he is not going to spend it twice on a thing he has not got a question about.** The seasonal outlook is still the last one, nobody has still asked for one in writing since May, and he is still not going to put a figure on it.

THE SENTENCE IS THERE, in the register and in Chapter 176, in both, and the register's quotation was never wrong. That is the fourth finding in this repository that has been wrong about the state layer rather than about the manuscript, and the rule is the same one in both directions: CHECK THE FILE.** **What the same review did find in the register was right, and is item 3 and item 11 above: the four-printed-sides claim and the description of the entry as being in a remarks column, and the register's cast entry had *MARO QUILL* in it and *HIS NAME IS NOW IN THREE PLACES ON THREE PRINTED SIDES AND IN ONE BOX THAT SAYS HE IS NOT THERE* in it, and all three are corrected.** **AND THE SAME IS TRUE OF *Alderquay*, WHICH THE REVIEW ALSO GOT BACKWARDS, AND IT WAS CAUGHT HERE AFTER BEING ACTED ON: the review said the Batch 0006 prompt *introduces Alderquay for the canon Alder Quay*. IT DOES NOT. *Alderquay* is the river-delta city and has been the city's name in `bible/terminology.md` and in `state/continuity.md` since Volume 01, and `outline/volume-03.md` lines 61 and 65 and `outline/series.md` line 230 all say *Alderquay's central flood gates*. *Alder Quay*, two words, is a different and much smaller thing: the river-core embankment, the Line 7 depot and the disused platform and the interchange a hundred and forty feet from the low end of the alley. Ninety-three instances of *Alder Quay* and thirty-nine of *Alderquay* stand in the manuscript and the two are not the same place. THIS REPAIR CHANGED THE BATCH 0006 PROMPT TO *ALDER QUAY* AND THEN REVERTED IT, because the review was wrong, and the revert is recorded here rather than left silent. THAT IS TWO FINDINGS IN FIFTEEN THAT WERE WRONG ABOUT THE STATE LAYER RATHER THAN ABOUT THE MANUSCRIPT, AND THE RULE IS THE SAME ONE IN BOTH DIRECTIONS: CHECK THE FILE, AND WHEN A FIX MAKES A PASS WORSE, REVERT IT AND WRITE DOWN WHY.**

**15. THE COUNTER-DENSITY FINDING IS RECORDED AND NOT ACTED ON, DELIBERATELY.** The reviewer noted that the block prints many fresh ordinals of the *Nth time* shape and flagged two that actually collide, which are items 8 and 9 above, and said the rest are load-bearing for Batch 0006. **The remaining instances — *fourth time this year* in Chapters 172 and 179, *twice in eight months* in Chapters 174 and 175, and *the other four wrong-shaped things in this book* in the Chapter 180 ledger — are left exactly as they are. ## CR-058 — Volume 03 Batch 0006 is written (Chapters 181–190): *The Village Or The Archive* — THE VOLUME'S CLIMAX, THE CHOICE PAID, A DIFFICULT FLOOD AT THE CENTRAL GATES, AND A PERMANENT LOSS OF THE SOUTH-BANK REMEMBERED-ROUTE MATERIAL

**1. NO NEW PREMISE, WORLD RULE, POWER RULE, ANTAGONIST LADDER OR FINAL ENEMY IS INTRODUCED, AND NO PLANNED ENDING IS ALTERED. NOTHING IS RESOLVED.** **Ten chapters, Saturday the thirty-first of October to Monday the thirtieth of November, thirty-one days. 35,076 words** — 4,310 / 3,200 / 2,824 / 2,685 / 3,070 / 2,801 / 3,825 / 3,091 / 4,354 / 4,916, every figure `wc -w` on the chapter files **after the writer's own verification pass, which is `CR-059` below.** What follows records what the manuscript fixed and what a later block, a reviewer or a repair pass must not undo. **Every figure is quotable from a chapter in `chapters/volume-03/chapter-0181.md` … `chapter-0190.md` by naming the file.** `outline/volume-03.md` was NOT edited and needs no change; `outline/series.md`, `outline/ending.md` and `outline/volume-01.md` were NOT edited. **THE VOLUME'S FINAL IMAGE — a village school painting its route on a wall in several colours, none of them official — IS NOT WRITTEN AND BELONGS TO CHAPTER 200. Volume 03 IS OPEN AND IS NOT CLOSED AND NO VOLUME-CLOSE PHASE EXISTS FOR IT. THE CONCRETE RESOLUTION BEGINS IN BATCH 0007 AND IS NOT IN THIS BLOCK.**

**2. THE CHOICE IS PAID, IN CHAPTER 181, BY HIM, ALONE, AND IT IS CORRECT AND HE IS NOT GLAD.** *He got on a city bus at about five past eight on Tuesday the third of November and he was in a village eleven miles the other way at about ten past ten and on the same bus back at about twenty past four and in eleven streets at about ten past six.* **THE DECIDING TOOK ABOUT FOUR SECONDS ON THE SATURDAY NIGHT OF THE THIRTY-FIRST OF OCTOBER WITH NOBODY IN THE ROOM, and the last ordinary days are Saturday, Sunday and Monday and the round went out on the Monday at ten past eight and came in at about four with an bag empty.** The reason, in his own hand: *a schedule is a thing that is either maintained or it is not maintained* — **which the manuscript now refers to as *the sentence at the top of the instrument*, and prints verbatim ONCE in the whole block, in `chapter-0181.md`, because the instrument's own words may not be repeated as a maxim** — *and in about nine weeks somebody is going to find out what it means to be a district that is not going to be maintained, and everybody in this city has been assuming that somebody means an office. It will not be an office. It will be a place where about a hundred and forty people live and a woman of thirty-four teaches, and it will be found out by them and not by anybody in Crown Heights.* **NOBODY PERSUADED HIM, NOBODY ASKED HIM, AND NO NEW FACE WALKED INTO A ROOM WITH A PROPOSAL IN IT.** He is not pleased, and the chapter says so, and it says that going is a thing a man does when he has decided, and that deciding took four seconds.

**3. BOTH PRICES WERE KNOWN BEFORE HE GOT ON THE BUS AND THE UNCERTAINTY IS PERMANENT.** Written in his own hand on the Monday night, in two unlabelled places under the two places: *going to the village is the only way I will find out what is in the drawer, because the only person who would tell me without my asking is a woman of fifty-four at the ninth desk on the left*; and *going to the drawer is the only way the village gets a number, and an objection has to be filed against something addressable*. **THE THING THAT WOULD TELL HIM IS STILL INSIDE ONE OF THE TWO AND HE STILL CANNOT KNOW WITHOUT GOING, AND HE WENT, AND HE MAY NOT BE PERMITTED TO FIND OUT AFTERWARDS WHETHER THE ARCHIVE ROUTE WOULD HAVE WORKED. `chapter-0186.md`: he went up the thirty-four steps on Friday the twentieth of November, did not ask, did not go near the drawer, and will not know. `chapter-0189.md` and `chapter-0190.md` both say the uncertainty is permanent and that in about four years somebody may go and get the sheet out and put it back and it will not be because of anything he did on any day.** The third door stayed shut: he did not ask Bettina Sallow a second question, did not write to her, and did not un-shut the door by any other road. **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT LEARN WHAT IS IN THE 1911 DRAWER FROM HIM, MAY NOT HAVE HIM GO BACK, MAY NOT HAVE HER TELL HIM, AND MAY NOT RESOLVE IT.**

**4. THE FLOOD, AND IT IS THE BLOCK'S SET PIECE, AND IT IS A DIFFERENT EVENT FROM THE ONE IN CHAPTERS 191–200.** **MONDAY THE TWENTY-THIRD OF NOVEMBER, AN ORDINARY MONDAY, IT HAD NOT RAINED FOR NINE DAYS, THERE WAS NO WIND, IT WAS ABOUT FOUR DEGREES, AND THE WATER CAME UP THROUGH A DRAIN AT THE END OF A LANE AT ABOUT HALF PAST TWO IN THE AFTERNOON AND FILLED THE LOW END OF BRIDGE STREET IN ABOUT ELEVEN MINUTES WITHOUT ARRIVING.** A foot in the road at the corner by about a quarter to three; about two inches by about seven; about four inches of grey silt on the sole of one door afterwards. **Nobody drowned, nobody was hurt, nobody was taken out of a house, nobody fought anything, and it was over in hours. About a hundred and forty people watched it. It was not staged at night and not staged in a storm.** **THE GATES ARE THE CITY'S AND ARE ABOUT A MILE AND A HALF FROM THE END OF THAT STREET AND HE HAS NEVER BEEN INSIDE THEM AND HAS NOT SEEN THEM, and he does not write down where they are on the page, and the reason is on the page: a description of a place is how a person gets found.** **THE MECHANISM, IN HIS OWN HAND: a gate is built to hold a difference in level between one side of it and the other, and a difference has to be a difference, and what came was the level on both sides going up together until the difference was about nothing, and a gate with no difference in it is not a gate, it is a hole in a wall with a door in it and about nine men who did exactly what their instructions said. NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT BUILT THE GATES AND NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT CAN BE ANGRY AT ANYBODY WHO OPERATES THEM, because not one of them has ever heard of that street, and a thing that fails while it is being operated correctly is not a fault, it is a limit, and there is no form in this city in which a limit can be entered.** **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT COLLAPSE THIS FLOOD WITH THE ONE IN CHAPTERS 191–200, MAY NOT PREVIEW IT, MAY NOT LET ANY CHARACTER IN THIS BLOCK FORESAY IT, MAY NOT MAKE A CHARACTER OF THIS BLOCK WRONG ABOUT IT, MAY NOT FIND ANYBODY TO BLAME, AND MAY NOT MOVE THIS ONE.**

**5. THE LOSS IS REAL, IT IS PERMANENT, AND IT IS NOT RECOVERED.** **THE DISTRICT'S OWN REMEMBERED-ROUTE MATERIAL FOR THE SOUTH BANK IS GONE, AND IT WAS NEVER ON PAPER, AND THE REASON IS ON THE PAGE AND IS NOT THAT A PAGE WAS WASHED AWAY.** The south bank is the far side of the bridge and the low ground behind Bridge Street, in eleven streets. What it consisted of, in three pieces, set out in `chapter-0189.md`: **about nine people who can get from the low end of Bridge Street to the old ford and back without going round, each of them a little differently, none of them written down and none of them ever asked; two mornings, a man of about fifty with a mower about nine feet onto the far half of the bridge on Tuesday the sixth of October, and a man of about sixty with a shed eleven minutes away at the end of a lane on Monday the ninth of November; and a field book with two pages on it that carry a time, a place and the words *I was here*.** **The Monday water moved the top of the path and cut the ditch about nine yards in and joined a hollow that was two yards south of where the hollow was, and he walked it twice on the Thursday and could find where the path had been because the grass on the old line is dead. IT IS NOT A DISTANCE, IT IS A SHAPE, and there is no form in this city in which a shape can be entered.** **WHY THE HALVES NO LONGER JOIN: they were never written down, they were in agreement because they were all on the same ground, and the only way to find out whether they still agree is to ask all nine, and the only reason they agreed is that nobody asked — so the one thing that would tell him is the thing that would destroy it.** Two of the nine cannot be asked and the reasons are structural: the man of about sixty with the shed **does not know that a piece of paper exists** and there is no answer to give him that is not a sentence about a person, and the mower man has said twice, in September and on the sixth of October, that he is not going to say it again and was right and the asking is the thing that would break what is holding. **There is no instrument in this city that can produce it: about nine hundred drawers of street numbers from 1911 whose named things are rateable things with a street on them; a schedule that wants a quantity, and a quantity is a length, and a length is a third kind of place and is not a remnant, and what is gone is not a length; a punch roll about nine inches long that knows how many and not who; a pad in a drawer in Crown Heights with four lines in it about about nine feet of drain; a roll in a woman's head; a jar with thirty-one years of wages in it. NONE IS GOING TO BE BUILT AND HE IS NOT GOING TO BE THE MAN WHO BUILDS ONE. A LATER BLOCK, INCLUDING THE VOLUME CLOSE, MAY NOT RECOVER IT, MAY NOT FIND A SURVIVOR WITH A COMPLETE COPY, MAY NOT FIND A SECOND SET OF PAGES, AND MAY NOT TURN IT INTO A LESSON.**

**6. THE CAUSATION IS ON THE PAGE AND IT IS NOT A PUNISHMENT AND IT IS NOT A BILL.** `chapter-0187.md` and `chapter-0189.md` and `chapter-0190.md`: *going to the village cost the drawer, and the drawer is the only thing that could have held the other thing, and the other thing is what is gone.* **AND HE MAY NOT BE PERMITTED TO SAY THE WORD BECAUSE, and the chapter says so in his own hand and does not say it: *I am not going to write the word because about any of this. There is a difference between a thing that happened and a thing that was paid for, and nobody in this street has been charged anything, and if I write because then I have turned a Tuesday into a bill and I have made that village into the paying party, and she is not, and I am not, and a Tuesday is the only day of the week I have and I spent it and it cost what it cost and the cost is not going to be on a schedule.*** **NOBODY IS TO BE TOLD THAT THE LOSS IS THE PRICE OF ANYTHING — not the man of sixty, not the mower man, not the woman of thirty-four, not the woman of fifty-four, not the woman of thirty-one, not a woman of sixty-one at a counter, not a woman of forty-six above a fitter's shop, not a foreman of fifty-four, AND NOT HIM, in a book that goes into a coat.** `chapter-0188.md` has her refuse to let him call it a cost as well, in a room, and neither of them said the word.

**7. THE FLOOD IS NOT A FIFTH OF THE FOUR, AND THE CHAPTER SAYS SO WITHOUT MAKING IT A FIFTH.** `chapter-0187.md` item three: *that is four and it is still four and this is not one of them, and the difference is on the page and it is about nine words long: one of the four had nobody in it, and this one had about a hundred and forty.* **AND THE OTHER HALF OF THE DIFFERENCE IS ON THE PAGE AND IS NOT THAT IT WAS WORSE: a thing that happened to a place and a thing that happened in front of about a hundred and forty people are different because the second one will be talked about on Friday, and about a hundred and forty people were not surprised because they have all seen it twice.** **There is no fifth, no total, no count, and he is not counting and has not counted since the first of July. NOBODY IN CHAPTERS 181–190 STANDS IN THE WEATHER ON A WEDNESDAY, and no chapter prints a total of anything. A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT TURN THE FLOOD INTO A FIFTH, MAY NOT START COUNTING THE FOUR, AND MAY NOT PRINT A RUNNING COUNT OF THE BLANK WEDNESDAYS.**

**8. SABRA TREWIN WAS ASKED, BADLY, AND ANSWERED ANYWAY, AND WAS NOT THANKED.** `chapter-0182.md`, Tuesday the third of November, in the schoolroom and on the step at about half past twelve: *Somebody has put your village on a list. I have not come to tell you what is on it, because I did not come to tell you what is on it, and I have not come to ask you to do anything about it either. What would you like me to do.* **BAD IN FOUR WAYS, ALL FOUR ON THE PAGE: it assumes he is a man who can do something; it is two questions wearing one coat; it gives her the whole of the decision in one move and takes the whole of the time out of it, and she had nine years of not being asked and he had about four hours and a bus; and he had the answer already.** She said *you have not asked me a question, you have handed me a job*, and then spoke for about four minutes, and the substance is on the page: she has not seen the paper and is not going to be shown it; **she is not frightened and she wants that said; there is no roll of paper in that room and there is not going to be one, and the day somebody asks for that piece of paper is the day the room is a place that can be asked about; and she has not told anybody in that village and is not going to.** And then: *you have not asked me a single question, I want that written down by somebody, and I do not care who.* **HE DID NOT WRITE IT DOWN AND THE REASON IS ON THE PAGE: a thing she said to one person is a thing two people know, and if it is in a book in a coat then three people know, and I am not going to be the second copy of the only piece of evidence she has got.** **He did not get a number, did not hand her a piece of stationery, did not give her a figure, did not write the four bells down and did not count the bell on the bracket, and did not ask her a second thing in this block or any other.** What it cost her is on the page and it is hers and permanent: a hundred and forty people and a decision to keep them out of it, alone, with about twenty children and a stove and a lath with a line burnt on it, and a man who came eleven miles and cannot help her carry it.

**9. THE FAILURE, IN A NEW PLACE, AND IT COSTS A MAN WHO CANNOT BE ASKED.** `chapter-0185.md`, Monday the sixteenth of November, four flights to the quay section office four rooms above a fitter's shop: **he had written a route on a sheet of his own paper in his coat on the Saturday in about forty minutes — eleven lines, seven places, four times, and one name at the end of it, a man of about fifty in a room with four machines — and he carried it upstairs and asked a woman of forty-six to look at it, and he had the sentence ready since the Saturday and he knew while he was saying it. THAT IS THE THIRD TIME IN EIGHT MONTHS HE HAS ARRIVED SOMEWHERE WITH THE WORDS ALREADY IN THE ORDER THEY ARE GOING TO BE SAID IN.** She corrected the satchel, told him the yard is not a place anybody can post to, said *you have got a man at the end of that*, said *take it out of the book*, and when he did not, said she would put it in the bin. **The finding, in his own hand and not improved: A ROUTE IS NOT A SEQUENCE. A ROUTE IS A THING YOU WALK AND IT DOES NOT HAVE A FIRST THING AND A SECOND THING. A SHEET WITH PLACES AND TIMES ON IT IS NOT A ROUTE, IT IS AN ORDER, AND A THING AT THE END OF AN ORDER IS A THING THAT WAS GOT TO, AND HE WAS NOT GOT TO.** And the cost: **a route that cannot be produced cannot be asked after, and he produced one, and there is no form anywhere for taking a produced route back out of production, and the man cannot be asked whether that was all right, because the only way to ask is to come back and the coming back is the second ask, and the first one was made on the twenty-first of September.** He did not go up the top track, did not ask the woman of thirty-four a second thing, did not ask the woman of fifty-four a second question, and did not put a name in a box. **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT HAVE HIM ASK THAT MAN ANYTHING, MAY NOT PUT THE SHEET ANYWHERE, MAY NOT HAVE NELL CRALE DESCRIBE IT, AND MAY NOT HAVE THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY LEARN THAT IT EXISTS.**

**10. THE CHANGED METHOD AND THE RECOVERY, BOTH SOMEBODY ELSE'S WORK, NEITHER A WORD, AND THE SOMEBODY HAS ALREADY REFUSED HIM ONCE.** `chapter-0186.md`, Friday the twentieth of November, a city bus at ten past ten and the thirty-four steps at about ten past eleven: **Bettina Sallow, fifty-four, ninth desk on the left. He stood at her desk for about ten minutes and did not ask her anything. She said two sentences, neither of them an answer: *you are not going to ask me* and *then don't stand at my desk… I have said the same thing twice and you have not asked me a third time and I want you to know that I have noticed that twice, and I am not going to thank you for it and I would rather you went down the stairs.*** He did not open the drawer, did not go near it, did not put his hand on it. **The changed method, in his own hand: I HAVE STOPPED ASKING AND I HAVE STARTED BEING REFUSED, AND A REFUSAL IS THE ONLY INSTRUMENT IN THIS CITY THAT CANNOT BE ASKED AFTER. SHE IS NOT GOING TO COME TO ELEVEN STREETS, HE IS NOT GOING TO ASK HER ANYTHING ELSE THIS YEAR, AND NOBODY IS GOING TO THANK HER. HE IS NOT CURED AND THE RECOVERY IS EIGHT WEEKS OLD AND IT IS HERS.** **AND HE MAY NOT BE CURED, MAY NOT THANK HER, MAY NOT ASK HER AGAIN, MAY NOT HAVE HER VISIT THE DISTRICT, AND MAY NOT HAVE HER SAY ONE WORD ABOUT WHAT IT DID.**

**11. THE COAT, AND IT IS THE BLOCK'S WORST-SHAPED THING.** `chapter-0186.md`: *a coat cannot be asked after, and that is the only reason anything survives in it, and it is the only reason anything in it can be produced, and the two of those are the same property and they cut in opposite directions, and there is no instrument anywhere in this city that can take a thing out of a coat and put it back where it cannot be asked for.* **There are two sheets in that inside pocket and neither is a record and neither is going to be a file and he is not going to post anything to anybody.**

**12. WHAT WAS GIVEN UP IN THIS BLOCK WENT ON NEITHER OF THE TWO LISTS.** The survey of the south bank — eleven items, not written down — given up on the far side of the bridge at about ten past one on Thursday the twenty-sixth, standing up, in about four seconds, because *a survey of a bank is a set of figures about a place, and the moment I have figures about a place the place is a place I have described, and a place I have described can be entered in a column, and I have watched that happen to eleven streets four times in eight months out of a contract.* **THE SIX OF HIS OWN ARE SIX AND THE SIX OF THE OTHER SHAPE ARE SIX AND THE TWO LISTS HAVE NOT BEEN MERGED, NEITHER HAS A SEVENTH, THERE IS NO THIRD LIST, AND A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT ADD A SEVENTH TO EITHER, MAY NOT COUNT THE THINGS GIVEN UP IN THIS BLOCK, AND MAY NOT CALL ANYTHING IN IT A SIXTH OF ANYTHING.** He also gave up a Tuesday at a gate in about four seconds (`chapter-0184.md`, *then I shall not stop*, and the van has not stopped at that gate on a Tuesday since the tenth of November) and gave up the archive by not going. **None of the three went on a list and no column was drawn and no fifth and no sixth.**

**13. THE RELATIONSHIP IS MILESTONE 3 AND IT IS PAID HERE AND IT IS NOT REPAIRED.** `chapter-0188.md`, Tuesday the twenty-fourth of November, in her room on a second floor about a mile from where the schedule was drafted — six desks, a counter, a window that opens, about nine people — **the sixth disagreement, unrepaired, colder, correct on both sides, and he loses.** Her half: *you have decided that the thing you did was a sacrifice and a sacrifice is a thing that has to be paid for by somebody, and you have nominated that village so that you do not have to pay it yourself*; and the romance's rule applied against him about a village for the second time; and *going and not going are not two halves of one thing, going is what un-does it*, said in a room and not in a corridor. His half: *going to the drawer is the only errand in this city that could have stopped that schedule and I am the only person who could have gone, and I knew that on the Monday night before I got on the bus.* **AND HE TOLD HER ABOUT THE SEASON, OUT LOUD, FOR THE FIRST TIME IN EIGHT MONTHS: I stopped counting the weeks in August, and I have not been able to tell you that, and I have said four sentences to you in four corridors and I have not said that one in any of them. And a date is a column and a column is worse than a count.** She said *thank you, that is all it is and I am not going to do anything with it*, and that she has had the right for about four months and he gave her nothing to do it with, and **SHE SAID SHE NOTICED IN SEPTEMBER THAT THE NUMBER HAD STOPPED GOING UP AND DID NOT TELL HIM AND DID NOT TELL HIM NOW AND IS NOT GOING TO.** **THE CUSTODY REGISTER PAGE IS UNTOUCHED FOR THE TWELFTH CONSECUTIVE BLOCK: in a drawer with a coat in it, not produced, not named, not confessed to, and neither of them said one word about it in about fifty minutes. He did not tell her about the sheet in the coat. She did not describe the line. THE LAST THURSDAY OF A MONTH IN THIS BLOCK IS THURSDAY THE TWENTY-SIXTH OF NOVEMBER, SHE RECONCILED THE RETURN ON IT, HE WAS NOT IN THE ROOM AND DID NOT GO AND DID NOT ASK, NOVEMBER HAS FOUR FRIDAYS SO THE RETURN HAS FOUR CELLS, DECEMBER HAS FOUR AS WELL, AND THE FIFTH-CELL RULE IS A RULE AND NOT A REASON. THE NEXT LAST THURSDAY IS THE THIRTY-FIRST OF DECEMBER AND IT IS NOT IN THIS BLOCK.** A later block may not repair this in this volume, may not make either of them warm, may not let either of them win it, may not have her describe the line on a reconciliation day, may not have him in the room, and may not have her tell him she noticed.

**14. THE TWO LISTS, THE PAIRS, THE LEDGER, THE COSTS, AND NONE OF IT MOVED BUT THE FIGURES.** Eleven remembered routes, ten crossings, ten tally lines, **no crossing staged in this block, so both figures stand.** The pairs are **twenty-nine** and have not moved since the night of the sixteenth of July — **one hundred and thirty-seven days at the end of the block** — with the reason on the page in eight Field Book entries and **NO ORDINAL PRINTED ANYWHERE.** The list is still two sixes, seven threes, one nine, four twos, three fives, one eight, one twenty-five, four sevens, six fours, and it must not be read as twenty-one, twenty-six, twenty-eight, thirty or thirty-one. The written line home is the three hundred and seventy-second day, blank, four marks in four hands, none of them his, **not cured, and its not being cured is not an absence of a cure.** The Lark token is cold on the three hundred and fifty-eighth day and has done nothing since the seventh of December. The first personal blank, the seam-scar, the record blank and the authorial blank appear nowhere in this block.

**15. THE SENSORY FAILURE RECURRED ONCE, IN CHAPTER 181, ON A PAVEMENT, AND IT ACQUIRED NOTHING.** At about half past two on Monday the second of November: the sound went, and under it, in the same four seconds, the taste he has never put a name to and has never put a place to, and it came and went in the same four seconds. **No face, no place, no person, no name, and it acquired none. IT DID NOT BECOME the brush, the trays, the Row, the barriers, the rack, the man of about sixty, a bus timetable, a schedule, four hundred and ten yards, a village, a bell, the name of a water, Neris, or a key. HE DID NOT WRITE THE TIME DOWN, WHICH IS THE THIRD TIME IN EIGHT MONTHS. IT MAY NOT BE CURED, MAY NOT ACQUIRE A FACE, A PLACE, A PERSON OR A NAME, MAY NOT BECOME NERIS OR A KEY, AND MAY NOT BECOME THE RAINWAY OR THE FLOOD — AND NONE OF THOSE IS WHAT IT BECAME, ON THE PAGE, ONCE.**

**16. NO ATLAS RESIDUE IN THIS BLOCK, AND THAT IS CORRECT.** The ten already used stand unchanged (Ch 66, 77, 89, 95, 108, 120, 130, 136, 149, 155) and **not one of the ten sentences appears in Chapters 181–190 and no new one was reached for.** A later volume may not repeat any of the ten.

**17. THE FOUR UNSHOWABLE THINGS, THE SEVENTEEN INHERITED ON-PAGE LISTS, THE BOUNDARIES, THE MARKS, THE POST, THE CHAIR, THE LEVY, THE QUERY AND EVERY OTHER STANDING CONSTRAINT HELD, AND NONE OF THEM IS RESTATED IN A CHAPTER.** Volume 02 is closed at Chapter 130 and nothing in this block discharges, restores, varies, withdraws, re-authors or re-lists any of it. **THE ELEVEN STREETS REMAINS ON EXACTLY FOUR PRINTED SIDES — the twentieth of August, the seventeenth of September, the first of October and the twenty-second of October — three of them naming it as a thing that is not going to be maintained and the fourth naming Cobb Lock, and none of them naming it as a place, and no fifth ground, no fifth figure, no counter-statement and no face behind the office of the First Surveyor.** Cobb Lock is District 214, 410 yd, ground (d), PROPOSED FOR WITHDRAWAL, person responsible *none of record*, **and that box is Cobb Lock's and is not Roy Pryce's and it does not move.** The third state is fixed. The order is suspended and not discharged, not varied and not withdrawn; the public name is not on the street name index; the classification of the eleventh is withdrawn and stays withdrawn; the removed sentence cannot be restored and the department cannot account for its removal after being asked twice, and the box went up empty a second time; the matter stands over, is not adjourned, has no date and is on no list; the line of the twelfth of January stands and its condition is not the department's; the levy is two and four a head a week in advance with no term on two hundred and fifty-two people in a building with no number and it is not curable, and **Lena Marr is not in this block and is not paid anything in it**; a woman of sixty on a second landing is unnamed and is the ninth of twenty-six names. **A round is one man short. A plate is in the grass and is never reported and is told to nobody. A cover has been flush since the sixth of April. An annual return goes in every January with its fifth box filled nil, a box asked about twice, and a sixth line of fifty-four words that nobody read this month and neither query was answered.** Ada Fenn is not named and not referred to in any of the ten chapters and the count of a woman of sixty-eight on nothing stands at eight. **The three clues from Volume 01 are three and unjoined. Three boundaries are three and are not joined and a flood is not a fourth kind of boundary. Three sets of marks are three and are not joined. Nobody has gone past the third post and nobody has levelled the handrail post.** The eleven streets' machinery is over: no gang, no sheet, no rack, no tin going anywhere, no satchel, the second tin at thirty-seven and no thirty-eight, nobody empties a rack, the post is the works' papers and it stopped, and **Monday the second of November was a Monday and there was no gang on it and the rack was not a rack day and the barriers were still out and the road was still open and both of those are correct.** Nobody asked a timetable clerk anything, nobody was sent anywhere, and the working timetable, the terminus column, the ruled margin headed *actual* with thirty-one names in it and the eight sentences that bind them were not queried, dated, explained or asked about. **Ada Fenn's name is written zero times.**

**18. NO NEW GRAMMAR, NO NEW FACTION, NO NEW ANTAGONIST, NO NEW FINAL ENEMY, AND NOBODY WALKED INTO A ROOM AS A PROBLEM.** Sel Iri does not appear. Halden Vey is not named. Orrin Sable does not appear. Tarin Kest does not appear. **SOREN ROOK IS NOT NAMED ANYWHERE IN CHAPTERS 1–190.** The First Atlas, the Singular Mode and the Quieting are not mentioned. **NOBODY WAS ADDED ABOVE CASS ARDERY, THE OFFICE OF THE PREFECT, OR A HEARING PANEL, AND NOTHING THAT WENT WRONG IN THIS BLOCK IS ATTRIBUTABLE TO A NEW FACE.** The people this block added to the page are ordinary and have jobs: a woman of thirty-four who teaches; a woman of forty-six at a counter above a fitter's shop; a woman of sixty-one at a clinic counter; a man of about fifty with a mower; a man of about sixty with a shed. **THE ANTAGONIST IS STILL THE COMMON ATLAS'S CULTURE OF SINGLE AUTHORSHIP IN MUNICIPAL FORM AND THIS BLOCK'S PROOF IS THE SAME MACHINERY DOING SOMETHING CORRECT FOR THIRTY-ONE DAYS AND A FLOOD.**

**19. WHAT A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT DO WITH ANY OF IT, IN ONE PLACE.** May not pay the choice again or make it by anybody else or have it be a working-harder answer or a too-late answer or a somebody-else-was-right answer. May not recover the south-bank material or soften it or find a survivor with a complete copy. May not let anybody be told it was the price of anything. May not learn what is in the 1911 drawer. May not ask Bettina Sallow anything, or write to her, or thank her, or have her come to the district. May not ask Sabra Trewin a second thing, or frighten her, or put her on a sheet, or have her be the woman who hands a district its first piece of stationery, or have her describe the roll. May not go up the top track, may not ask Barnaby Pike anything, may not put the sheet in a coat anywhere. May not ask the man of about sixty with a shed anything, or tell him a piece of paper exists. May not have the mower man say the boundary thing a third time, and may not have him be asked about the flood. May not have the man of about fifty in the room with four machines learn that the sheet exists. May not have Nell Crale describe the sheet. May not have the foreman read the sixth line, may not query line five, may not answer either query, may not ask him about December. May not merge the two lists, may not add a seventh to either, may not draw a fifth or a sixth column, and may not make a third list. May not print a fifth of the four unshowable things, may not count them, may not print a total, and may not have anybody stand in the weather on a Wednesday. May not use Atlas residue. May not add a boundary, a mark, a clue, a grammar, a faction, an antagonist or a final enemy. **May not write the volume's final image, which belongs to Chapter 200.**
